Bootstrap drops 204 → 136 LOC (-68 LOC\n90\t  net after header expansion).\n91\t\n92\t  **Step 3**: Reorder `.claude/settings.json` SessionStart chain — append\n93\t  `continue-injector-spawn.sh` as the 18th and LAST hook in the\n94\t  startup|resume|clear matcher chain (after `harness-health-self-scan.sh` at\n95\t  current index 17). Order is critical: the spawn-induced chain truncation can\n96\t  no longer suppress any downstream hook because there ARE no downstream hooks.\n97\t\n98\t  **Step 4**: Author companion firing-test\n99\t  `scripts/hooks/firing-tests/continue-injector-spawn-fire-test.sh` (~210 LOC,\n100\t  9 TCs) per Phase 3.5 Hard Rule #2:\n101\t  - TC1: source=compact (out-of-scope) → exit 0, NO log entry\n102\t  - TC2: source=clear, autonomous_mode=false, no override → SKIPPED\n103\t  - TC3: source=clear, autonomous_mode=false, FORCE_CONTINUE_ON_CLEAR=1 → FIRED (override path)\n104\t  - TC4: source=clear, autonomous_mode=true → FIRED\n105\t  - TC5: source=resume → FIRED unconditionally\n106\t  - TC6: source=startup, autonomous_mode=false → SKIPPED (race risk)\n107\t  - TC7: source=startup, autonomous_mode=true → FIRED\n108\t  - TC8: missing current-execution.md → autonomous defaults false → SKIPPED\n109\t  - TC9: empty payload (no source field) → exit 0, NO log entry\n110\t\n111\t  **Step 5**: Production verification — bootstrap smoke-test confirms\n112\t  additionalContext + .session-ready still emit cleanly (no spawn); spawn-hook\n113\t  smoke-test confirms FIRED log line emits with correct FIRE_REASON. Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\n8\tset -uo pipefail\n9\ttrap 'exit 0' ERR\n10\t\n11\tPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\n12\tSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\n13\tmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\n14\tHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\n15\tHEALTH_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health.log\"\n16\tCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-cache-${SID}\"\n17\tTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n18\t\n19\t# === Same-session cache check (5-min TTL) ===\n20\tif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n21\t  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n22\t  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n23\t  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n24\t    echo \"[$TS] harness-health-self-scan: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n25\t    exit 0\n26\t  fi\n27\tfi\n28\t\n29\t# === Aggregation counters ===\n30\tHIGH=0\n31\tMEDIUM=0\n32\tLOW=0\n33\tSKIP=0\n34\tFAIL_DETAILS=\"\"\n35\t\n36\temit_fail() {\n37\t  local signal=\"$1\" sev=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n38\t  case \"$sev\" in\n39\t    HIGH) HIGH=$((HIGH + 1)) ;;\n40\t    MEDIUM) MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + 1)) ;;\n41\t    LOW) LOW=$((LOW + 1)) ;;\n42\t  esac\n43\t  FAIL_DETAILS=\"${FAIL_DETAILS}${signal}=${sev}(${detail});\"\n44\t  printf '[%s] %s sev=%s detail=%s session=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$sev\" \"$detail\" \"$SID\" >> \"$HEALTH_LOG\"\n45\t}\n46\t\n47\temit_skip() {\n48\t  SKIP=$((SKIP + 1))\n49\t  printf '[%s] %s SKIP=%s session=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$1\" \"$2\" \"$SID\" >> \"$HEALTH_LOG\"\n50\t}"}]},"uuid":"1537716e-724a-4913-9f9c-c727f51a39a6","timestamp":"2026-05-09T05:30:41.884Z","toolUseResult":{"type":"text","file":{"filePath":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge\\scripts\\hooks\\harness-health-self-scan.sh","content":"#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# harness-health-self-scan.sh — continuous harness-health verification.\n# Implements 12-signal catalog HH-1..HH-12 codified in agent-workspace/{proposals,constitution}/harness-health-protocol.md\n# (currently at proposals/; mv to constitution/ pending M-S173-1 deny-lift workaround).\n# Triggers: UserPromptSubmit (every prompt; same-session caching) + SessionStart (each session entry).\n# Phase 3.5 T6 deliverable; D-035 ratified S173 via AskUserQuestion Q3=A.\n# <2s perf budget on UserPromptSubmit. Exit 0 ALWAYS (informational, not blocking).\nset -uo pipefail\ntrap 'exit 0' ERR\n\nPROJECT_DIR=\"${CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR:-.}\"\nSID=\"${CLAUDE_SESSION_ID:-unknown}\"\nmkdir -p \"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory\"\nHOOK_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.session-hooks.log\"\nHEALTH_LOG=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health.log\"\nCACHE=\"$PROJECT_DIR/agent-workspace/memory/.harness-health-cache-${SID}\"\nTS=\"$(date -Iseconds)\"\n\n# === Same-session cache check (5-min TTL) ===\nif [ -f \"$CACHE\" ]; then\n  CACHE_MTIME=$(stat -c %Y \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || stat -f %m \"$CACHE\" 2>/dev/null || echo 0)\n  AGE=$(( $(date +%s) - CACHE_MTIME ))\n  if [ \"$AGE\" -lt 300 ]; then\n    echo \"[$TS] harness-health-self-scan: CACHE-HIT age=${AGE}s session=$SID\" >> \"$HOOK_LOG\"\n    exit 0\n  fi\nfi\n\n# === Aggregation counters ===\nHIGH=0\nMEDIUM=0\nLOW=0\nSKIP=0\nFAIL_DETAILS=\"\"\n\nemit_fail() {\n  local signal=\"$1\" sev=\"$2\" detail=\"$3\"\n  case \"$sev\" in\n    HIGH) HIGH=$((HIGH + 1)) ;;\n    MEDIUM) MEDIUM=$((MEDIUM + 1)) ;;\n    LOW) LOW=$((LOW + 1)) ;;\n  esac\n  FAIL_DETAILS=\"${FAIL_DETAILS}${signal}=${sev}(${detail});\"\n  printf '[%s] %s sev=%s detail=%s session=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$signal\" \"$sev\" \"$detail\" \"$SID\" >> \"$HEALTH_LOG\"\n}\n\nemit_skip() {\n  SKIP=$((SKIP + 1))\n  printf '[%s] %s SKIP=%s session=%s\\n' \"$TS\" \"$1\" \"$2\" \"$SID\" >> \"$HEALTH_LOG\"\n}","numLines":50,"startLine":1,"totalLines":340}},"sourceToolAssistantUUID":"c7ea86f3-e45f-418c-bd94-6f47aa97a41e","userType":"external","entrypoint":"cli","cwd":"C:\\htdocs\\stockforge","sessionId":"1b47a593-5d74-420c-a786-c9e8507a8942","version":"2.1.126","gitBranch":"main"}
